SIFT — NOTICE

SIFT: To-Do List
Copyright 2026 Jung-geun Bong

The original SIFT source code, documentation, and project assets are licensed
under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (SPDX: Apache-2.0). See LICENSE.

Third-party software, model weights, tokenizers, and assets retain their own
copyrights and licenses. They are not relicensed by SIFT's Apache-2.0 grant.
This NOTICE supplies attribution and license-scope information; it does not
modify the Apache License or replace any upstream license or notice.

Third-party software
--------------------
Remote Swift package sources and exact versions/revisions are recorded in:
  ios/todo.xcodeproj/project.xcworkspace/xcshareddata/swiftpm/Package.resolved
Locally patched MLX Swift 0.32.3 and MLX Swift LM 3.32.3 sources, original
licenses, upstream revisions and modification records are retained in:
  ios/Vendor/mlx-swift/SIFT-PROVENANCE.json
  ios/Vendor/mlx-swift-lm/SIFT-PROVENANCE.json
Other dependencies include FluidAudio, WhisperKit, Apple Swift packages,
Hugging Face Swift packages, EventSource, and yyjson. Their own LICENSE/NOTICE
files remain applicable. An SDK license does not establish the license of
model weights downloaded through that SDK.
The iOS app includes full pinned SDK license and notice texts, including
vendored and embedded-library attributions, in ios/todo/ThirdPartyNotices.txt.
Open them in the app via Settings > App Info > Open-Source Licenses.

Backend, Android, and Python dependencies likewise retain their own licenses.
Their manifests and lockfiles identify the dependencies; a license field on
SIFT's own package does not change any dependency's license. The Apache notices
in android/gradlew and android/gradlew.bat remain intact.

--------------
The icons in docs/assets/icons are licensed under the MIT License.
Copyright (c) 2023 Phosphor Icons
The complete copyright and permission notice is retained in:
  docs/assets/icons/LICENSE

App Store marketing assets
--------------------------
docs/assets/images/app-store-badge.svg is the unmodified Korean App Store
download badge supplied by Apple. It remains Apple's marketing artwork,
not an original SIFT asset licensed under Apache-2.0. Use is governed by:
  https://developer.apple.com/app-store/marketing/guidelines/

  https://tools.applemediaservices.com/api/badges/download-on-the-app-store/black/ko-kr?size=250x83
The app-*.webp screen images reproduce SIFT's public App Store screenshots:
  https://apps.apple.com/kr/app/sift-to-do-list/id6782752844
Apple's trademarks and interface assets retain their respective rights.

Downloaded model artifacts
--------------------------
Downloaded models and tokenizers are third-party material, not original SIFT
code. Consult the license and notices for the actual artifact and upstream
weights before using, modifying, or redistributing them, including commercially.
The current source references:

- MLX Community MiniCPM5 and Qwen conversions:
  https://huggingface.co/mlx-community/MiniCPM5-1B-4bit
  https://huggingface.co/mlx-community/Qwen3.5-2B-MLX-4bit
  https://huggingface.co/mlx-community/Qwen3.5-4B-MLX-4bit
  These identifiers are not a license grant or an immutable revision pin.

- Laya Core ML conversion and original multilingual checkpoint:
  https://huggingface.co/FluidInference/laya-coreml/tree/7b8d7a2b7e28e746c6ecaad44bbcd5cf251a4fcc
  https://huggingface.co/convaiinnovations/laya/tree/1c5edc17a7acd8701df6fc341c0d179f1c62c982
  The conversion and original weights have separate provenance and license scope.

- Nemotron Core ML diarization conversion:
  https://huggingface.co/FluidInference/nemotron-3-diarization-coreml/tree/25a90f97f254428d4b30374b76af9c74fdee8327
  The Community-1 attribution below does not establish Nemotron's license.

- WhisperKit Core ML conversion of OpenAI Whisper large-v3-turbo:
  https://huggingface.co/argmaxinc/whisperkit-coreml/tree/0f63a7800b00dd0226abd051b906c246e1907482
  Model-weight and tokenizer terms remain separate from the WhisperKit SDK.

Community-1-derived speaker helper models
----------------------------------------
SIFT references FBank.mlmodelc and Embedding.mlmodelc from the pinned
Fluid Inference Community-1 artifact set. These are modified Core ML
conversions, not original SIFT models or fine-tuned models. The upstream
notice scopes CC BY 4.0 to its exact supported Community-1 artifacts; it does
not cover unrelated Nemotron models or the repository's legacy assets.

Attribution: pyannote, WeSpeaker, BUT Speech@FIT (Brno University of Technology),
and Fluid Inference. Fluid Inference converted the PyTorch components to
Core ML, configured fixed/enumerated input shapes, separated the FBank
frontend from the embedding backend, and compiled Apple-platform packages.
The upstream notice additionally identifies BUT Speech@FIT's PLDA material;
this attribution is not a claim that SIFT uses every upstream pipeline asset.

License: Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International (CC BY 4.0)
  https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/
Source pipeline:
  https://huggingface.co/pyannote/speaker-diarization-community-1
Exact conversion notice, provenance, and citations:
  https://huggingface.co/FluidInference/speaker-diarization-coreml/blob/df2625ac79a7ac6b65ad868fee6d80f320da4232/NOTICE.md
  https://huggingface.co/FluidInference/speaker-diarization-coreml/blob/df2625ac79a7ac6b65ad868fee6d80f320da4232/README.md

Upstream citations retained for attribution:
- Alexis Plaquet and Herve Bredin. Powerset multi-class cross entropy loss
  for neural speaker diarization. INTERSPEECH, 2023.
- Hongji Wang, Chengdong Liang, Shuai Wang, Zhengyang Chen, Binbin Zhang,
  Xu Xiang, Yanlei Deng, and Yanmin Qian. Wespeaker: A research and production
  oriented speaker embedding learning toolkit. ICASSP, pages 1-5, 2023.
- Federico Landini, Jan Profant, Mireia Diez, and Lukas Burget. Bayesian HMM
  clustering of x-vector sequences (VBx) in speaker diarization: theory,
  implementation and analysis on standard tasks. Computer Speech & Language,
  2022.

The Apache License does not grant trademark rights or transfer signing keys,
provider credentials, developer accounts, or permission to use hosted services.

FluidAudio/ThirdPartyLicenses/NemoTextProcessing-LICENSE.md
Version: 0.17.4
Source: https://github.com/FluidInference/FluidAudio/tree/21493f8dac5a97e65742e6ff26f42f164c2fda0f

# NemoTextProcessing (text-processing-rs)

The `NemoTextProcessing` binary target is the prebuilt xcframework of
[`text-processing-rs`](https://github.com/FluidInference/text-processing-rs)
(v0.3.0), used by `NemoTextNormalizer` for byte-exact NeMo text normalization.

- **Source:** https://github.com/FluidInference/text-processing-rs
- **License:** Apache License, Version 2.0

Built with the `fst-engine` feature, the xcframework includes and statically
links the following third-party works. All are permissive (Apache-2.0 / MIT)
and compatible.

## NVIDIA NeMo Text Processing

The compiled weighted-FST grammars and text-normalization fixtures are derived
from / copied from NVIDIA NeMo Text Processing (pinned commit
`1f1263579fe57ba7ed783cad3dddee710fcc5064`).

- **Source:** https://github.com/NVIDIA/NeMo-text-processing
- **License:** Apache License, Version 2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) NVIDIA CORPORATION & AFFILIATES.

## rustfst

Loads and executes the compiled OpenFST grammars.

- **Source:** https://github.com/Garvys/rustfst
- **License:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) Alexandre Caulier and the rustfst contributors.

## flate2

Decompresses the bundled gzipped grammars at load time.

- **Source:** https://github.com/rust-lang/flate2-rs
- **License:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) Alex Crichton and the flate2 contributors.

## Other Rust dependencies

The xcframework transitively links additional permissive-licensed Rust crates
(MIT and/or Apache-2.0) via `rustfst` and `flate2` — e.g. `nom`,
`miniz_oxide`, `bitflags`, `anyhow`. See the crate's `THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.md`
for detail.

Full texts: Apache-2.0 <https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0>,
MIT <https://opensource.org/license/mit>.


===== SIFT-LICENSE-SECTION =====
FluidAudio/ThirdPartyLicenses/KokoroAneSpanishFrenchG2P-LICENSE.md
Version: 0.17.4
Source: https://github.com/FluidInference/FluidAudio/tree/21493f8dac5a97e65742e6ff26f42f164c2fda0f

# Spanish / French text frontends (Kokoro ANE `.spanish`, `.french`)

`fr_lexicon_cache.json` and `es_lexicon_cache.json` on
`FluidInference/kokoro-82m-coreml` are generated from word lists in ipa-dict
(https://github.com/open-dict-data/ipa-dict, `fr_FR` and `es_ES`), plus
proper nouns from Misaki's English lexicons. Their pronunciations were
generated with espeak-ng and Misaki's `EspeakG2P` post-processing, the
phonemization Kokoro's Spanish and French voices were trained on. No
espeak-ng code is distributed. The French file keeps ipa-dict's
h aspiré marks. Words missing from the French lexicon go through CharsiuG2P
(https://github.com/lingjzhu/CharsiuG2P, MIT), already shipped as
`MultilingualG2PEncoder/Decoder.mlmodelc`.

FluidAudio/ThirdPartyLicenses/JapaneseG2P-LICENSE.md
Version: 0.17.4
Source: https://github.com/FluidInference/FluidAudio/tree/21493f8dac5a97e65742e6ff26f42f164c2fda0f

# Japanese text frontend (Kokoro ANE `.japanese`)

The Japanese text → IPA frontend is a Swift port of Misaki's `cutlet.py`
(hexgrad/misaki, Apache-2.0; itself adapted from polm/cutlet, MIT) and
Misaki's `num2kana.py` (copied from Greatdane/Convert-Numbers-to-Japanese,
MIT), and ships Misaki's `ja_words.txt` word list (Apache-2.0).

The MeCab dictionary it tokenizes with is unidic-lite
(https://github.com/polm/unidic-lite), a redistribution of UniDic 2.1.2 under
the BSD license, trimmed to the `pos1,pron,kana` fields by
`mobius/models/tts/kokoro/coreml/g2p/convert_unidic_lite.py`.

## unidic-lite / UniDic — BSD

and is released under any of the GPL (see the file GPL), the LGPL (see
the file LGPL), or the BSD License (see the file BSD).

NemoTextProcessing / text-processing-rs v0.3.1 — NOTICE
Source: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/FluidInference/text-processing-rs/v0.3.1/NOTICE

text-processing-rs
Copyright 2026 FluidInference

This product is a Rust port of NVIDIA NeMo Text Processing
(https://github.com/NVIDIA/NeMo-text-processing), which is licensed
under the Apache License, Version 2.0.

   Copyright (c) NVIDIA CORPORATION & AFFILIATES.
   Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0.

In addition, when built with the optional `fst-engine` feature, this
product includes and redistributes artifacts derived from NVIDIA NeMo
Text Processing (Apache-2.0, pinned commit
1f1263579fe57ba7ed783cad3dddee710fcc5064):

   * the compiled weighted-FST grammars under `grammars/` (exported from
     NeMo's Pynini source), and
   * the text-normalization test fixtures under `tests/fixtures/`
     (copied from, or regenerated as the deterministic output of, NeMo's
     `data_text_normalization` test cases).

This product's binary distributions (e.g. the published xcframework) also
statically link third-party libraries. See THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.md for the
full list and their licenses.


===== SIFT-LICENSE-SECTION =====
NemoTextProcessing / text-processing-rs v0.3.1 — THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.md
Source: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/FluidInference/text-processing-rs/v0.3.1/THIRD-PARTY-LICENSES.md

# Third-Party Licenses

`text-processing-rs` is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (see
`LICENSE` and `NOTICE`). Its distributions include or link the following
third-party works. All are permissive (Apache-2.0 / MIT) and compatible with
this crate's license.

---

## NVIDIA NeMo Text Processing

- **Used for:** the port's grammar logic, and — under the `fst-engine` feature —
  the compiled weighted-FST grammars in `grammars/` and the test fixtures in
  `tests/fixtures/`, which are derived from or copied from NeMo
  (pinned commit `1f1263579fe57ba7ed783cad3dddee710fcc5064`).
- **Source:** https://github.com/NVIDIA/NeMo-text-processing
- **License:** Apache License, Version 2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) NVIDIA CORPORATION & AFFILIATES.

---

## rustfst

- **Used for:** the `fst-engine` feature — loading and executing the compiled
  OpenFST grammars (composition + tropical shortest-path). Statically linked
  into `fst-engine` binary distributions.
- **Source:** https://github.com/Garvys/rustfst
- **License:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) Alexandre Caulier and the rustfst contributors.

---

## flate2

- **Used for:** the `fst-engine` feature — decompressing the bundled gzipped
  grammars at load time. Statically linked into `fst-engine` binary
  distributions.
- **Source:** https://github.com/rust-lang/flate2-rs
- **License:** MIT OR Apache-2.0
- **Copyright:** Copyright (c) Alex Crichton and the flate2 contributors.

---

## Other Rust dependencies

`fst-engine` transitively links additional Rust crates via `rustfst` and
`flate2` (e.g. `nom`, `miniz_oxide`, `bitflags`, `anyhow`), and the default
build links `lazy_static`. All are distributed under permissive licenses
(MIT and/or Apache-2.0). See each crate's entry on <https://crates.io> and the
generated `Cargo.lock` for exact versions.

Full texts of the Apache License 2.0 and the MIT License are available at
<https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0> and
<https://opensource.org/license/mit>.
